# Capture days (Stage 2)

Generated 2026-11-20T09:38:40 by `Tools/VanderbiltCampus/pipeline/capture_days.py` from [SHOT-LIST.md](SHOT-LIST.md) / `config/shot_list.json`. Each day is a loop that starts and ends at **S** on its map. Times: walking 1.2 m/s between stops, 4 s per photo, 360 legs at 1 m/s with two passes. Storage: 25 MB per ProRAW/DNG photo, 8 MB per HEIC/JPEG.

## Summary

- Main days (hero + normal): **74**, 217 h, 160,339 photos, 4,008 GB as DNG / 1,283 GB as HEIC.
- Optional later days (background: hospital, parking, utility, walls facing out of campus): **20**, 58 h, 40,345 photos, 1,009 GB DNG / 323 GB HEIC.
- Day length: 2.49-3.37 h (target 2.6 h).
- Not scheduled: 478 blocked facades (nothing to photograph).
- 360 legs are timed as 360 walks; with a phone only, use the photo counts given (one photo every 1.5-2 m). If you use a 360 camera, 8K video is about 15 MB per second (~1,455 GB for every 360 entry, two passes).
- **Session**: morning or afternoon, whichever fits more of the day's shade-side photos. Entries listed under the other session are best on an overcast day (or swap them with a later day).
